# Document index - Caelum Argenteum

Generated by `build_document_index.py`. Do not edit this file manually.
It indexes maintained documents under `docs/` with strictly more than 5000 words (whitespace-separated tokens). Exactly 5000 words does not qualify. This file is a locator, not a substitute for reading the applicable rules.

Regenerate after an indexed source changes, is renamed or crosses the threshold in either direction:

```text
python build_document_index.py
```

## Search and read commands

List the indexed documents:

```text
python build_document_index.py list
```

List section entries matching a document or topic:

```text
python build_document_index.py list "prisoner"
```

Read a selected inclusive line range from the original:

```text
python build_document_index.py read SYSTEMS.md 3643 3676
```
